### P0.6-05 (branch claude/p0-6-05, 21 Oct 2026, Rashid's session 11 of the bundled plan, Opus)
- `vercel.json`: one `/(.*)` rule with HSTS (max-age 63072000, includeSubDomains, preload), nosniff, `strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()`, and `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` (self; scripts from Sentry's CDN, Turnstile and Meta; the Supabase project over https and wss; Sentry ingest; Turnstile frames; `frame-ancestors 'none'`, `base-uri 'self'`, `form-action 'self'`, `object-src 'none'`; `report-uri` to Sentry project `kabsi-web`, id 4512145411801168, read from the loader script). PostHog needs no entry: it is same-origin through `/ingest`. `'unsafe-inline'` stays for scripts and styles while report-only (the boot script and the hydration data); nonces come with enforcement. `Cache-Control: no-store` on `/app`, `/staff`, `/start`, `/login`, `/a/*`, `/activate/*`, `/api/*`.
- Turnstile with a server check: sign-up and login by email code (`auth-code`, already), the Profile Check (`profile-snapshot`, already), the review link tool (`review-link`, already), the contact form (`lead`, already; no page sends it yet), and now Nora: `assistant` refuses a new conversation from a visitor who is not signed in without a valid token (`challenge_failed`, 400); the widget shows the Cloudflare check until a conversation exists and sends the token once. Live before this change: a call with no token to `profile-snapshot`, `review-link` and `lead` answers 400 `challenge_failed` (the secret is set). The partner form is after launch (R-42). Google sign-in has no form to protect.
- Rate limits: `review-link` now 10 a minute per IP before anything else (plus 20 an hour and 400 a day as before); `profile-snapshot` already 10 a minute, checked before Turnstile. Live burst on `profile-snapshot` from one address (pg_net, 15 calls): 10 x 400 then 5 x 429. From this container the 15 calls spread over 6 rate-limit keys because the outbound address rotates; a forged `X-Forwarded-For` is not trusted (no key change).
- Vercel Firewall (the edge in front of kabsi.co; Cloudflare only holds DNS, so its WAF and bot rules do not see kabsi.co traffic): Bot Protection managed ruleset switched on in **log** mode with the Vercel connector (the update returned success; reading the config back was refused by this session's permission check, so Rashid reads it back). Log first so uptime checks and the session's own curl checks are not challenged; switch to challenge after a week of logs.
- Tests: `tests/security-headers.test.ts` (7). Checks: typecheck, eslint and prettier on the changed files, `npm test` (33 files, 199 tests), `npm run build`, `deno check` on `assistant` and `review-link`. After that the sheet is a tracker: Reported to Google, Google answered, Still showing, Removed, Appeal sent, with only the next step offered, and the appeal step (its own checklist and the same tool link) offered once. The sheet and the steps say that Google decides and that Kabsi cannot remove a review or promise Google will.
- Google's pages, read 21 Oct 2026 (the date is in the sheet and in `src/lib/review-report.ts`): the Maps content policy (`support.google.com/contributionpolicy/answer/7400114`, "Prohibited & restricted content") for the categories, and "Report or flag a review" (`support.google.com/business/answer/4596773`) for the steps, the tool, the statuses, the one appeal ("typically takes several days"; Google does not step into disputes between a business and a customer). The category list is Google's own order and names; Kabsi's plain line for each is its own.
- Data: migration `20261021220000_review_reports` (applied by the session, additive; no delete or drop in it): `public.review_reports` (one per review; the category as a short key, the ticked evidence keys, the state and its dates; no review text, no name, no evidence text), RLS read for members and staff, writes only through `start_review_report`, `advance_review_report` and `list_review_reports` (owner or manager; Pro checked in the function through `feature_access`; the order of states and one appeal enforced in the function). The two foreign keys with cascade clauses are in `supabase/migrations/20261021220010_review_reports_links.sql` for Hussein (After merge). Until he runs it nothing breaks; a deleted business or review would leave its report behind.

"The deep link opens (URL tested)": `tests/review-report.test.ts` parses `REVIEWS_TOOL_URL` (host support.google.com, path `/business/workflow/9945796`); a person should click it once (After merge). ### P0.8-07 (branch claude/h-p0-8-07, 21 Oct 2026, Hussein's session 8 of the bundled plan, Sonnet)
- What it is: **Make a graphic** on a 4 or 5 star review that still has words (the full card for a review that needs a reply, and the read-only view of the others). A bottom sheet offers a square (1080 x 1080) and a story (1080 x 1920), makes the square at once, shows the image, and offers Download and, where the phone allows it, Share. Kabsi posts nothing. A business that is not on Pro sees "Review graphics are part of Pro." with a link to Plan, and no image is made.
- Why Satori and resvg in an Edge Function (the card asked the chat to pick and record): an Edge Function keeps the Pro, role and rating checks on the server (a browser-only canvas could not enforce Pro), keeps the review text inside Kabsi's own database and the one request, and needs no new service. Everything the renderer needs is embedded (`_shared/review-graphic/assets.ts`: resvg-wasm gzipped, Readex Pro 400 and 700 and Lalezar 400 as woff, 1.3 MB), so it has no network or file dependency at run time. Satori reads woff, not woff2, so the fonts are the same OFL families the website serves, in woff, from `@fontsource`. Cost: none beyond the function call (no model, no paid API).
- Rules in code (`_shared/review-graphic/text.ts`): only 4 and 5 stars; first name and initial ("Maria K."; no name or a non-Latin one is "A customer"); links, bare domains, emails, phone-like digit runs, hashtags, handles and emoji removed; em and en dashes become a hyphen and exclamation marks become full stops (Kabsi's house style); cut at a space no earlier than character 120 and no later than 200, trailing punctuation dropped, then an ellipsis, so no word is broken. Arabic and other non-Latin text is refused with a plain message: Satori shapes Arabic letters but lays the words out in the wrong order (tested with a spike), so a wrong image is worse than none.
- Checks the function makes in order (`_shared/review-graphic/handler.ts`): signed in (401), owner or manager of the review's business (404 for staff, a stranger or a missing review, so existence is not revealed), 40 graphics an hour per person (429, fail closed), Pro through `public.feature_access` asked again on the server (403 `pro_required`), rating and words (422). Nothing is stored (no Storage, no table); the log line is `{ok, size}` only; review text and names are never logged.
- Done when: "a 4-star and a 5-star review produce both sizes and a 3-star one is refused": Deno `render.test.ts` (real PNGs: signature bytes and width and height 1080 x 1080 and 1080 x 1920 for 4 and 5 stars) and `text.test.ts` and `handler.test.ts` (a 3 star review gets 422 `rating` and nothing is rendered). "A 600-character review is cut at about 200 with an ellipsis and no mid-word break": `text.test.ts` (length at most 201, at least 150, ends with the ellipsis, the last word is whole). "A review with a phone number or link is cleaned": `text.test.ts` (numbers, emails, www, https, bare .lb and .com, hashtags and handles gone). - Deploy run 5 (37185310050, merge of PR 20, commit 2a204f9): Edge Functions job success, Worker kabsi-go job failure. Log line: "Missing entry-point: The entry-point should be specified via the command line ... or the `main` config field." The four runs on 29 Sep had the same shape (Edge Functions success, Worker failure).
- Cause: `cloudflare/wrangler-action@v3` installs Wrangler 3.90.0 by default, which does not read `workers/kabsi-go/wrangler.jsonc`. Reproduced with `wrangler deploy --dry-run` on a clean copy of the folder: 3.90.0 fails with the same error; 3.114.17 bundles the Worker (3.41 KiB) and lists the STICKERS binding and vars.
- Fix: PR 21 pins `wranglerVersion: "3.114.17"` in `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` (merged as 7b82144). Deploy run 6 (37185695836) on 7b82144: both jobs success. This was the first Worker deploy from CI, so the Worker code on main is now live on go.kabsi.co; the TAP_SECRET set in the Cloudflare dashboard is untouched by `wrangler deploy`.
- Edge Function versions after run 6 (`list_edge_functions`): `api` 34, `content` 27, `assistant` 21, all updated 2026-10-04 about 07:25 UTC, after the PR 20 merge.
- Mock-review check on Yawmiyati (location 9803ee99-fee8-4c6a-abb4-1a830fcbb438, mock mode): `staff_mock_review` needs a staff login that the Supabase connector does not have, so the same row it writes was inserted into `mock_google_reviews` (review id mock-eafac0a35ce9446684800827a9285d6e, 3 stars, reviewer "Deploy check", 07:27:37 UTC). The cron synced it at 07:30:03 (reviews row e3852d23-d5ff-4253-829c-001e0f61df61, state drafted, one reply draft) and sent the email at 07:30:08: `emails` row 84558298-d9f7-4d72-8f19-02b99d59f869, kind review_new, status sent, subject "New review for Yawmiyati (3 of 5)", to rashid.hamzy@gmail.com, resend_id 01a105d1-f845-7ee8-b09d-3a13d23eb9db.
- Wording in the deployed code (`get_edge_function` for `api`, searched for strings): "Review reply" 2 hits, "Nothing is published until you approve it." 1, "trademarks of Google LLC" 1, "Kabsi is operated by Hussein Slim, Dubai, United Arab Emirates" 1; "Beirut, Lebanon", "only posts what you approve", "Put mine back" and ">Post<" 0 hits.
- Plan correction: the `emails` table has no body column (columns: id, location_id, partner_id, kind, to_address, subject, resend_id, status, error, dedupe_key, created_at). The plan's "check the `emails` row body" (P0.1-02b Done when, and the standard prompt) cannot be done. The check is the sent email (row exists with status sent, then read the inbox) plus the wording in the deployed function code.
- Test data clean-up (applied on Hussein's "apply", 4 Oct 2026, one statement): deleted 3 `action_tokens` rows for the review, the `reviews` row e3852d23-d5ff-4253-829c-001e0f61df61 (its one `reply_drafts` row went with it, `on delete cascade`) and the `mock_google_reviews` row mock-eafac0a35ce9446684800827a9285d6e; the statement returned 3, 1 and 1 deleted rows. A read-back shows 0 rows in `mock_google_reviews`, `reviews`, `reply_drafts` and `action_tokens` for those ids. The `emails` row 84558298-d9f7-4d72-8f19-02b99d59f869 is kept as the record of the send.
